Lemma 7
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{4}^{2}=~

\displaystyle m(m+1)\left[p\left(p+2\right)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}+\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\right)+p\left(\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)}+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\right)\right]+2mp(n+p+1)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\frac{(n-h)m^{2}p^{2}}{n}\left[1+o(1)\right],\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\Xi_{4}
is defined in (
20
).

Proof.

For the conciseness of notation, we define

\bm{\Gamma}
as
\mathbf{X}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}
and
hence have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{4}^{2}=\mathbb{E}{\mathbf{W}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{\Gamma}\mathbf{W}\mathbf{W}_{i}}.\vskip 2.84526pt

We begin the discussion by expanding
\mathbf{W}\mathbf{W}_{i}
as

\displaystyle\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}\\
\mathbf{W}_{2}^{\top}\\
\cdots\\
\mathbf{W}_{n}^{\top}\end{bmatrix}\mathbf{W}_{i}=\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\\
\mathbf{W}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\\
\cdots\\
\mathbf{W}_{n}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\end{bmatrix}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Then we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{4}^{2}=~

\displaystyle\sum_{s=1}^{n}\sum_{t=1}^{n}\ \Gamma_{ij}\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\mathbf{W}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\right)\left(\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\right)\right]=\Gamma_{ii}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\right)^{2}+\underbrace{\sum_{s\neq i}\sum_{t\neq i}\Gamma_{st}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\right)}_{\sum_{s\neq i}\Gamma_{ss}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{W}_{s}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\right)^{2}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\Gamma_{ii}\mathbb{E}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{m}W_{ij}^{2}\right)^{2}+\sum_{s\neq i}\Gamma_{ss}\cdot m=m(m+1)\mathbb{E}\Gamma_{ii}+m\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{\Gamma}).

(61)

We can thus complete the proof by separately computing

\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})
and
\mathbb{E}\Gamma_{ii}
.
First we compute
\mathbb{E}\Gamma_{ii}
, which proceeds as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Gamma_{ii}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)^{2}+\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}p(p+2)+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)}p+\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}p(p+2)+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}p

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle p\left(p+2\right)\left[\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}+\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\right]+p\left[\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)}+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\right].

(62)

Then we turn to the computation of
\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})
, which proceeds as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{\Gamma})=~

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\right\|}_{2}^{2}+\sum_{s\neq\pi^{\natural}(i),j}\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{s}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\right\|}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{2}^{4}+2\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{2}+\ 2\sum_{s\neq\pi^{\natural}(i),j}\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{s}\right\|}_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 2p(p+3)+2(n-2)p=2p(n+p+1).

(63)

The proof is thus completed by combing
(
61
), (
63
),
and (
62
).
∎

Lemma 8
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{1}\Xi_{4}=~

\displaystyle\frac{mp(n-h)(n+p-h)}{n}\left[1+o(1)\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\Xi_{1}
and
\Xi_{4}
are defined in
(
20
).

Proof.

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{1}\Xi_{4}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\underbrace{\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}^{\natural\top}\mathbf{X}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}}_{\triangleq\bm{v}^{\top}}\mathbf{W}\mathbf{W}_{i}}.\vskip 2.84526pt

First we conditional on
\mathbf{X}
.
Expanding the product
\mathbf{W}\mathbf{W}_{i}
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\begin{bmatrix}\mathbf{W}_{1}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\\
\mathbf{W}_{2}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\\
\cdots\\
\mathbf{W}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\\
\cdots\\
\mathbf{W}_{n}^{\top}\mathbf{W}_{i}\end{bmatrix}=\begin{bmatrix}0\\
0\\
\cdots\\
m\\
\cdots\\
0\end{bmatrix},\vskip 2.84526pt

we can compute
\mathbb{E}\Xi_{1}\Xi_{2}
w.r.t.

\mathbf{W}
as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\Xi_{1}\Xi_{2}\right)=\mathbb{E}\bm{v}^{\top}\mathbf{W}\mathbf{W}_{i}=m\mathbb{E}\bm{v}_{i},\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\bm{v}_{i}
denotes the
i
-th entry of
\bm{v}
and can be written as

\displaystyle\bm{v}_{i}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\Lambda_{1}}+\underbrace{\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{\top}\bm{\Delta}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\Lambda_{2}}.\vskip 2.84526pt

For
\Lambda_{1}
, we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{1}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}+\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}

\displaystyle-~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}(p+3)\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M})-\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}(p+1)\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(n-h)\left(\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}(p+3)-\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}(p+1)\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})=\frac{p(n-h)^{2}}{n}\left[1+o(1)\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Then we turn to
\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}
and obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=~

\displaystyle\ \mathbb{E}\underbrace{\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\bm{\Delta}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\Lambda_{2,1}}+\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\Delta}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\Lambda_{2,2}}

\displaystyle-~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\Delta}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\Lambda_{2,3}}-\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\bm{\Delta}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\Lambda_{2,4}}.\vskip 2.84526pt

We compute the value of
\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}
under the four
different cases.

Case
(s,s)
:
i=\pi^{\natural}(i)
and
j=\pi^{\natural}(j)
.

In this case, we have
\bm{\Delta}
be

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}=\bm{\Delta}^{(s,s)}=\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}.\vskip 2.84526pt

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,1}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=(p+2)(p+5)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,2}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=2(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,3}=~

\displaystyle\ \mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,4}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=0,\vskip 2.84526pt

which implies

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=(p+2)\left(p+7\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Case
(s,d)
:
i=\pi^{\natural}(i)
and
j\neq\pi^{\natural}(j)
.

First we write
\bm{\Delta}
as

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{(s,d)}=\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Then we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,1}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=(p+2)(p+4)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,2}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,3}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,4}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=0,\vskip 2.84526pt

which suggests that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=(p+2)(p+5)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Case
(d,s)
:
i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)
and
j=\pi^{\natural}(j)
.

In this case,
\bm{\Delta}
reduces to

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{(d,s)}=\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}.\vskip 2.84526pt

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,1}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{0}=(p+2)\left[1+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,2}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{p\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{0}=\left(p+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,3}=~

\displaystyle 0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,4}=~

\displaystyle 0,\vskip 2.84526pt

which suggests

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=2(p+1)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\left(p+3\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Case
(d,d)
:
i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)
and
j\neq\pi^{\natural}(j)
.

In this case,
\bm{\Delta}
is written as

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{(d,d)}=\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}.\vskip 2.84526pt

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,1}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}};

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,2}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}p(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}p\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right]}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}p(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{j=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left\|\mathbf{X}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}};

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,3}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{0}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{0}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(j)}p\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{0};

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,4}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{0}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{0}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(j)}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}_{0}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Hence we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=~

\displaystyle 2(p+1)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}2\left(p+1\right)^{2}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(j)}(p+1)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}(p+3)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}(3p+5)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

∎

Lemma 9
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{2}\Xi_{3}=~

\displaystyle\ \frac{p(n-h)(n+p-h)}{n}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\left[1+o(1)\right],\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\Xi_{2}
and
\Xi_{3}
are defined in (
20
).

Proof.

To start with, we write the expectation as
\mathbb{E}\Xi_{2}\Xi_{3}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{2}\Xi_{3}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{B}^{\natural}}_{\bm{u}^{\top}}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\underbrace{\mathbf{X}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\bm{p}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times 1}}\mathbf{W}_{i}^{\top}\underbrace{\mathbf{B}^{\natural\top}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}^{\natural\top}\mathbf{X}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\bm{v}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\mathbf{W}_{i}^{\top}\bm{v}=\mathbb{E}\left\langle\mathbf{W}_{i},\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\bm{v}\right\rangle.\vskip 2.84526pt

Exploiting the independence among
\mathbf{X}
and
\mathbf{W}
, we condition on
\mathbf{X}

and have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{W}}\left\langle\mathbf{W}_{i},\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\bm{v}\right\rangle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{W}}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{i}}\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\bm{v}\right).\vskip 2.84526pt

Note that only the diagonal entries of the Hessian
matrix
\nabla_{\mathbf{W}_{i}}\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\bm{v}
matters.
For an arbitrary index
s
, we can compute
the gradient of the
s
-th entry of
\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\bm{v}
w.r.t.

\mathbf{W}_{i,s}
as

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dW_{i,s}}\left(\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\bm{v}_{s}\right)=\bm{v}_{s}\dfrac{d}{dW_{i,s}}\left(\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\right)=\bm{v}_{s}\sum_{t=1}^{n}\dfrac{d}{dW_{i,s}}\left(\bm{p}_{t}\mathbf{W}_{t}^{\top}\bm{u}\right)=\bm{v}_{s}\dfrac{d}{dW_{i,s}}\bm{p}_{i}\mathbf{W}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}=\bm{p}_{i}\bm{v}_{s}\bm{u}_{s}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Invoking the definitions of
\bm{p},\bm{v}
and
\bm{u}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{W},\mathbf{X}}\left\langle\mathbf{W}_{i},\bm{u}^{\top}\mathbf{W}^{\top}\bm{p}\bm{v}\right\rangle=\mathbb{E}_{\mathbf{X}}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\sum_{s=1}^{m}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{B}^{\natural\top}\right)_{s}\left(\mathbf{B}^{\natural\top}\right)^{\top}_{s}\mathbf{X}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}^{\natural\top}\mathbf{X}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\small{\raisebox{0.6pt}{\textcircled{\raisebox{-.8pt}{1}}}}}}{{=}}~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\right]}_{\Lambda_{1}}+\ \mathbb{E}\underbrace{\left[\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}-\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)\right]}_{\Lambda_{2}},\vskip 2.84526pt

where in
\raisebox{-.8pt}{1}⃝
we use the
relation
\sum_{s=1}^{m}\left(\mathbf{B}^{\natural\top}\right)_{s}\left(\mathbf{B}^{\natural\top}\right)_{s}^{\top}=\mathbf{B}^{\natural}\mathbf{B}^{\natural\top}=\mathbf{M}
.

For the first term
\Lambda_{1}
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{1}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}}

\displaystyle-~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}-\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{p\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(n-h)\left[\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}(p+3)-(p+1)\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Then we consider the second term
\Lambda_{2}
, which can be decomposed
further into four sub-terms reading as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\Lambda_{2,1}}+\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\Lambda_{2,2}}

\displaystyle-~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\Lambda_{2,3}}-\mathbb{E}\underbrace{\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Delta}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\Lambda_{2,4}}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Case
(s,s)
:
i=\pi^{\natural}(i)
and
j=\pi^{\natural}(j)
.

In this case, we have
\bm{\Delta}
be

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{(s,s)}=\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Hence we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,1}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{X}_{i}\right]=\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{4}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}+\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(p+2)(p+4)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,2}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{X}_{j}\right]=\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}+\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{j}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 2\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=2(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,3}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{X}_{j}\right]=0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,4}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{X}_{i}\right]=0,\vskip 2.84526pt

which suggests

\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=\left(p+2\right)\left(p+7\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})
.

Case
(s,d)
:
i=\pi^{\natural}(i)
and
j\neq\pi^{\natural}(j)
.

First we write
\bm{\Delta}
as

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{(s,d)\top}=\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Then we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,1}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\right)}_{\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{4}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\right)}_{0}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\right)}_{0}=\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{4}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=(p+2)(p+4)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M});

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,2}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{0}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{0}=\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}=(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,3}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{X}_{j}\right]=0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,4}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\left(\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{X}_{i}\right]=0,\vskip 2.84526pt

which suggests
\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=(p+2)(p+5)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})
.

Case
(d,s)
:
i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)
and
j=\pi^{\natural}(j)
.

In this case,
\bm{\Delta}
reduces to

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{(d,s)\top}=\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Then we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,1}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{0}=\left(1+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\right)(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,2}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{4}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}p\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right]}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{0}=\left(p+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,3}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{X}_{j}\right]=0,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,4}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\right)\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right]=0,\vskip 2.84526pt

which suggests

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=2(p+1)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+(p+3)\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Case
(d,d)
:
i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)
and
j\neq\pi^{\natural}(j)
.

In this case,
\bm{\Delta}
is written as

\displaystyle\bm{\Delta}^{(d,d)\top}=\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}+\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,1}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(p+2)\left[1+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}+\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,2}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{4}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+p\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right]}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{4}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\kern-1.20552pt\left|\mathbf{X}_{i}\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|\kern-1.20552pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle 2\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}p(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+p\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\left[\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}2(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq j}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right],

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,3}=~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{0}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{0}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{p\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(j)}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}\right)}_{0},

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2,4}=~

\displaystyle\ \underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{0}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{0}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(j)}\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(j)}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}_{j}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{j}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural-1}(j)}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}\right)}_{0},\vskip 2.84526pt

which gives

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Lambda_{2}=~

\displaystyle(p+1)\left[2+2(p+1)\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(j)}\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\left[p+3+(3p+5)\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

∎

B.3
Supporting Lemmas

We study the high-order expectations of Gaussian random vectors’ inner product, which hopefully
will serve independent interests.

Lemma 10
.

Assume
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
and
\bm{y}\in\mathbb{R}^{p}
are Gaussian distributed
random vectors whose entries follow the i.i.d. standard normal distribution, then we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right)=~

\displaystyle\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

(64)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{y}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)=p\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

(65)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}=~

\displaystyle\ \left[\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right]^{2}+\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M})+\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right),

(66)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})=~

\displaystyle(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

(67)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{4}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})=~

\displaystyle(p+2)(p+4)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),

(68)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\ \left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}=~

\displaystyle\left(p+4\right)\left[\left(\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right)^{2}+\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M})+\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right)\right],

(69)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{4}\ \left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}=~

\displaystyle\left(p+4\right)\left(p+6\right)\left[\left(\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right)^{2}+\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M})+\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right)\right],

(70)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{y})^{2}\bm{y}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{1}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}\bm{y}=~

\displaystyle 2\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}_{1})\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}_{2})+\left(p+4\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}_{1}\mathbf{M}_{2})+2\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}_{1}\mathbf{M}_{2}^{\top}),

(71)

where
\mathbf{M}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}
is a fixed matrix.

Remark 6
.

If we assume
\mathbf{M}=\mathbf{I}_{p\times p}
, we can get

\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{4}=p(p+2)
,

\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{6}=p(p+2)(p+4)
,
and
\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{8}=p(p+2)(p+4)(p+6)
.

Proof.

This lemma is proved by iteratively applying
the Wick’s theorem in Theorem
3
,
Stein’s lemma in Lemma
19
,
and Lemma
18
.

Proof of (
64
) and (
65
)
.
The proof can be conducted easily with the property such that

\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{u}\bm{v}^{\top})=\bm{u}^{\top}\bm{v}=\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{v}\bm{u}^{\top})
holds for arbitrary
vectors
\bm{u}
and
\bm{v}
.

•

Proof of (
66
)
.
This property is a direct consequence of
Neudecker and Wansbeek 1987
(Equation
(3.2)
), which is attached in
Lemma
18
for the sake of self-containing.

•

Proof of (
67
)
.
Invoking the Stein’s lemma, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})=\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{x}}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\bm{x}\right].\vskip 2.84526pt

Then our goal transforms to computing the trace of the Hessian matrix

\operatorname{Tr}\left[\nabla_{\bm{x}}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\bm{x}\right]
.
For the
i
-th entry of the gradient,
we have

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}=\left\langle\mathbf{M}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle+\langle(\mathbf{M}^{\top})_{i},\bm{x}\rangle,\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\mathbf{M}_{i}
is the
i
-th row (or column) of
\mathbf{M}
. Then we obtain

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[x_{i}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\right]=\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}+x_{i}\left[\left\langle\mathbf{M}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle+\langle(\mathbf{M}^{\top})_{i},\bm{x}\rangle\right],\vskip 2.84526pt

and hence

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})=~

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})+\sum_{i=1}^{p}\mathbb{E}\left[x_{i}\left(\left\langle\mathbf{M}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle+\langle(\mathbf{M}^{\top})_{i},\bm{x}\rangle\right)\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle p\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+2\sum_{i}\mathbf{M}_{ii}=(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

•

Proof of (
68
)
.
Following the same strategy as in proving (
67
), we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\bm{x}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{4}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})=\mathbb{E}\left[\nabla_{\bm{x}}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\bm{x}\right].\vskip 2.84526pt

Then our goal transforms to computing the trace of the Hessian matrix

\operatorname{Tr}\left[\nabla_{\bm{x}}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\bm{x}\right]
.
For the
i
-th entry of the gradient, we obtain

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[x_{i}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\right]=

\displaystyle{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\cdot(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})+x_{i}\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\cdot(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})+2x_{i}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})+x_{i}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\left[\left\langle\mathbf{M}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle+\langle(\mathbf{M}^{\top})_{i},\bm{x}\rangle\right],\vskip 2.84526pt

whose expectation reads as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\cdot(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})+2\mathbb{E}\left[x_{i}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\right]+\mathbb{E}x_{i}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\left[\left\langle\mathbf{M}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle+\langle(\mathbf{M}^{\top})_{i},\bm{x}\rangle\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+2\mathbb{E}\left[x_{i}^{2}M_{ii}x_{i}^{2}+x_{i}^{2}\left(\sum_{j\neq i}M_{jj}x_{j}^{2}\right)\right]+2M_{ii}\mathbb{E}\left[x_{i}^{2}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+2M_{ii}\left(\mathbb{E}x_{i}^{4}\right)+2\sum_{j\neq i}M_{jj}(\mathbb{E}x_{i}^{2})(\mathbb{E}x_{j}^{2})+2M_{ii}\left[\mathbb{E}(x_{i}^{4})+\sum_{j\neq i}(\mathbb{E}x_{i}^{2})(\mathbb{E}x_{j}^{2})\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+6M_{ii}+2\sum_{j\neq i}M_{jj}+2M_{ii}\left(3+p-1\right)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(p+4)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+2(p+4)M_{ii}.\vskip 2.84526pt

Then we conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left[\nabla_{\bm{x}}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\bm{x}\right]=~

\displaystyle p(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+2(p+4)\sum_{i}M_{ii}+2p\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(p+2)(p+4)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

•

Proof of (
69
).

Invoking the Stein’s lemma, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}=~

\displaystyle\sum_{i}\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[x_{i}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}\right]=p\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}+4\sum_{i}x_{i}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle.\vskip 2.84526pt

The proof is then completed by invoking
Lemma
11
.

Proof of (
70
).

Following the same strategy as in proving (
69
),
we consider the

i
-th gradient w.r.t
x_{i}
, which can be written as

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{4}\ \left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}=\sum_{i}\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[x_{i}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\sum_{i}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}+2\sum_{i}x_{i}^{2}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}+4\sum_{i}x_{i}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})},\bm{x}\right\rangle

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\sum_{i}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}+2\sum_{i}\mathbb{E}\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[x_{i}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}\right]+4\sum_{i}\mathbb{E}\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle\right].

(72)

Noticing the following relations

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[x_{i}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}\right]=~

\displaystyle\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)^{2}+4x_{i}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle,

(73)

\displaystyle\dfrac{d}{dx_{i}}\left[{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle\right]=~

\displaystyle\ 2x_{i}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle 2{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle^{2}+M_{ii}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right),

(74)

we can conclude the proof by combining
(
69
),
(
72
), (
73
),
(
74
),
and Lemma
11
.

Proof of (
71
).

Due to the independence between
\bm{x}
and
\bm{y}
, we first condition
on
\bm{x}
and have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}(\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{y})^{2}\bm{y}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{1}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}\bm{y}=\ \mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}}\mathbb{E}_{\bm{y}}\bm{y}^{\top}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{y}\bm{y}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{1}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}\bm{y}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\small{\raisebox{0.6pt}{\textcircled{\raisebox{-.8pt}{1}}}}}}{{=}}~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\right)\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}_{1}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}\right)+\mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{1}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}\right)+\mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}^{\top}\bm{x}\bm{x}\mathbf{M}_{1}^{\top}\right)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}}{\left\|\bm{x}\right\|}_{2}^{2}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}\mathbf{M}_{1}\bm{x}+\ \mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{1}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}\bm{x}+\ \mathbb{E}_{\bm{x}}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{1}^{\top}\bm{x}\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}_{2}^{\top}\bm{x}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\small{\raisebox{0.6pt}{\textcircled{\raisebox{-.8pt}{2}}}}}}{{=}}~

\displaystyle 2\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}_{1})\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}_{2})+\left(p+4\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}_{1}\mathbf{M}_{2})+2\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}_{1}\mathbf{M}_{2}^{\top}),\vskip 2.84526pt

where in
\raisebox{-.8pt}{1}⃝
and
\raisebox{-.8pt}{2}⃝
we both use
Lemma
18
.

∎

Lemma 11
.

For a fixed matrix
\mathbf{M}\in\mathbb{R}^{p\times p}
, we associate it with
a symmetric matrix

\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}
defined as
\nicefrac{{(\mathbf{M}+\mathbf{M}^{\top})}}{{2}}
. Consider
the Gaussian distributed random vector
\bm{x}\sim\mathsf{N}(\bm{0},\mathbf{I})
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sum_{i}x_{i}(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x})\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle=\left(\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right)^{2}+{\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\kern-1.72218pt\left|\mathbf{M}\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|\kern-1.72218pt\right|}_{\textup{F}}^{2}+\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Proof.

This lemma is a direct application of Wick’s theorem, which
is completed by showing

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}x_{i}\left(\bm{x}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\bm{x}\right)\left\langle\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i},\bm{x}\right\rangle=\mathbb{E}\sum_{j}\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}M^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i,j}M_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}x_{i}x_{j}x_{\ell_{1}}x_{\ell_{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle}}{{=}}~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\sum_{j}\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\ell_{1}=i}\mathbbm{1}_{\ell_{2}=j}M^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i,j}M_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}x_{i}x_{j}x_{\ell_{1}}x_{\ell_{2}}}_{\sum_{j}M^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i,j}M_{i,j}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\sum_{j}\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{\ell_{2}=i}\mathbbm{1}_{\ell_{1}=j}M^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i,j}M_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}x_{i}x_{j}x_{\ell_{1}}x_{\ell_{2}}}_{\sum_{j}M^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i,j}M_{j,i}}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\sum_{j}\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{\ell_{1}=\ell_{2}}M^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i,j}M_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}}x_{i}x_{j}x_{\ell_{1}}x_{\ell_{2}}}_{\sum_{\ell}M^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i,i}M_{\ell,\ell}}=\sum_{j}2\left[M^{(\mathsf{sym})}_{i,j}\right]^{2}+M_{i,i}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\mathbf{M}^{(\mathsf{sym})}
is defined as
\left(\mathbf{M}+\mathbf{M}^{\top}\right)/2
.
∎

Then we study the properties of
\bm{\Sigma}
, which is defined as

\mathbf{X}^{\top}\bm{\Pi}^{\natural}\mathbf{X}-\bm{\Delta}
.

Lemma 12
.

For a fixed matrix
\mathbf{M}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\right)=n^{2}\left[\frac{p}{n}+\left(1-\frac{h}{n}\right)^{2}+o(1)\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),\vskip 2.84526pt

where matrix
\bm{\Sigma}
is defined in (
23
).

Proof.

We conclude the proof by showing

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}^{\top}\right)\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\small{\raisebox{0.6pt}{\textcircled{\raisebox{-.8pt}{1}}}}}}{{=}}~

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}\in{\cal S}}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}^{\top}\right]+\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}\in{\cal D}}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell_{1})}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell_{2})}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in{\cal S}}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\right)\ +\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}\in{\cal S},\ell_{1}\neq\ell_{2}}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}^{\top}\right]

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in{\cal D}}\ \underbrace{\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\right]}_{p\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}+\sum_{(\ell_{1},\ell_{2})\in{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left[\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}^{\top}\right]}_{\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(n-h)(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+(n-h)(n-h-1)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+hp\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+|{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}|\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\small{\raisebox{0.6pt}{\textcircled{\raisebox{-.8pt}{2}}}}}}{{=}}~

\displaystyle n^{2}\left[\frac{p}{n}+\left(1-\frac{h}{n}\right)^{2}+o(1)\right]\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}),\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\raisebox{-.8pt}{1}⃝
is due to the definitions of index sets

{\cal S}
and
{\cal D}
(Equation (
28
) and in
Equation (
29
)), and
\raisebox{-.8pt}{2}⃝
is because
|{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}|\leq h
.
∎

Lemma 13
.

For a fixed matrix
\mathbf{M}
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M})=\left(n-h+|{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}|\right)\left[\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}\right)\right]^{2}+(n-h)^{2}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M}\right)+n\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M}^{\top}\right)\vskip 2.84526pt

Proof.

Following the same strategy as in proving Lemma
12
,
we complete the proof by showing

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}(\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M}\bm{\Sigma}\mathbf{M})=~

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\right)+\ \sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}\in{\cal S},\ell_{1}\neq\ell_{2}}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{1}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}\mathbf{X}_{\ell_{2}}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right)

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in{\cal D}}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right)+\sum_{\ell\in{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}}\mathbb{E}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right)

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle\left(n-h+|{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}|\right)\left[\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}\right)\right]^{2}+(n-h)^{2}\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M}\right)+n\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M}^{\top}\right).\vskip 2.84526pt

∎

Lemma 14
.

For a fixed matrix
\mathbf{M}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\operatorname{Tr}(\Sigma\mathbf{M})\right]^{2}=\ (n-h)^{2}\left[\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right]^{2}+n\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right)+\left(n-h+\left\lvert{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}\right\rvert\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Proof.

We complete the proof by showing

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\operatorname{Tr}(\Sigma\mathbf{M})\right)^{2}=~

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in{\cal S}}\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{X}^{\top}_{\ell}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\right)^{2}+\sum_{\ell_{1},\ell_{2}\in{\cal S},\ell_{1}\neq\ell_{2}}\left(\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right)^{2}+\sum_{\ell\in{\cal D}}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{M}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}}_{\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}^{\top}\mathbf{M})}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell\in{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}}\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}}_{\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M})}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(n-h)^{2}\left[\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\right]^{2}+n\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{M}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\right)+\left(n-h+\left\lvert{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}\right\rvert\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

∎

Lemma 15
.

For a fixed matrix
\mathbf{M}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sum_{\ell=\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}=~

\displaystyle(n-h)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+(p+1)\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

Proof.

Provided that
i=\pi^{\natural}(i)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{1,1}=~

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{i}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}+\sum_{\ell\neq i,~\ell=\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{i}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{i}

\displaystyle=~

\displaystyle(p+2)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})+\sum_{\ell\neq i,~\ell=\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})=\left(n-h+p+1\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural}(i)}.

(75)

Provided that
i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\Xi_{1,1}=~

\displaystyle\sum_{\ell=\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}\mathbb{E}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}=(n-h)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M})\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)}.

(76)

Combining (
75
) and (
76
) then completes the proof.
∎

Lemma 16
.

For a fixed
\mathbf{M}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\sum_{\ell}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(i)}^{\top}\mathbf{M}\mathbf{X}_{\pi^{\natural}(\ell)}\mathbf{X}_{\ell}^{\top}\mathbf{X}_{j}=\left(p\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i\neq\pi^{\natural}(i)}+\mathbbm{1}_{j=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}\right)\operatorname{Tr}(\mathbf{M}).\vskip 2.84526pt

We omit its proof as it is a direct application of Wick’s theorem
(Theorem
3
).

Lemma 17
.

We have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbbm{1}(i=\pi^{\natural}(i))=~

\displaystyle\frac{n-h}{n}(1+o_{\textup{P}}\left(1\right)),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}=~

\displaystyle\frac{h}{n^{2}}(1+o_{\textup{P}}\left(1\right)),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbbm{1}_{j=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}=~

\displaystyle\frac{h}{n^{2}}(1+o_{\textup{P}}\left(1\right)),

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\mathbbm{1}_{i=j}\mathbbm{1}_{i=\pi^{\natural 2}(i)}=~

\displaystyle\ \frac{|{\cal D}_{\textup{pair}}|}{n^{2}}(1+o_{\textup{P}}\left(1\right)).\vskip 2.84526pt

This lemma can be easily proved by assuming
the indices
i
,
j
,
\pi^{\natural}(i)
,
and
\pi^{\natural}(j)
are uniformly sampled from
the set
\{1,2,\cdots,n\}

Appendix C
Useful Facts

This section collects some useful facts for the
sake of self-containing.

Theorem 3

(Wick’s theorem (Theorem
1.28
in
Janson 1997
))
.

Considering the centered jointly normal variables

g_{1},g_{2},\cdots,g_{n}
, we
conclude

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(g_{1}g_{2}\cdots g_{n}\right)=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\textup{all possible disjoint}\\
\textup{pairs }(i_{k},j_{k})\in\left\{1,2,\cdots,n\right\}\end{subarray}}\prod_{k}\mathbb{E}\left(g_{i_{k}}g_{j_{k}}\right).\vskip-5.69054pt\vskip 2.84526pt

With Wick’s theorem, we can reduce the computation of
high-order Gaussian moments to calculating the expectations of
a series of low-order Gaussian moments.

Lemma 18

(Equation
(3.2)
in
Neudecker and Wansbeek 1987
)
.

For a normally distributed random matrix
\mathbf{G}\in\mathbb{R}^{n\times p}
which satisfies

\mathbb{E}\mathbf{G}=\bm{0}
and
\mathbb{E}\textup{vec}(\mathbf{G})\textup{vec}(\mathbf{G})^{\top}=\mathbf{U}\otimes\mathbf{V}
,
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left(\mathbf{G}^{\top}\mathbf{A}\mathbf{G}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{G}^{\top}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{G}\right)=\

\displaystyle\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{U}\right)\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{B}\mathbf{U}\right)\mathbf{V}\mathbf{C}\mathbf{V}+\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{B}^{\top}\mathbf{U}\right)\mathbf{V}\mathbf{C}^{\top}\mathbf{V}

\displaystyle+~

\displaystyle\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{A}\mathbf{U}\mathbf{B}\mathbf{U}\right)\operatorname{Tr}\left(\mathbf{C}\mathbf{V}\right)\mathbf{V},\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\textup{vec}(\cdot)
is the vector operation;
\otimes
is the
Kronecker product
(
Horn and Johnson 1990
)
; and
\mathbf{A},\mathbf{B}
and
\mathbf{C}

are arbitrary fixed matrices.

Lemma 19

(Stein’s Lemma (cf. Section
1.3

in
Talagrand 2010
))
.

Let
g\sim\mathsf{N}(0,1)
. Then for any
differentiable function
f:\mathbb{R}\mapsto\mathbb{R}
we have

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[gf(g)]=\mathbb{E}f^{{}^{\prime}}(g),\vskip 2.84526pt

where
\lim_{\|g\|\rightarrow\infty}f(g)e^{-a\|g\|^{2}_{2}}=0

for any
a>0
.
